	public static bool CanKillTile(int i, int j, SpecialKillTileContext context)
	{
		if (context == SpecialKillTileContext.MowingTheGrass)
		{
			Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
			if (tile == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (tile.type == 2 || tile.type == 109)

	public static bool CanKillTile(int i, int j, out bool blockDamaged)

67	function flipBlock(
68	  w: Wiring, x: number, y: number,
69	  blockW: number, blockH: number, tilesW: number, tilesH: number,
70	  axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number, delta: number,
71	): void {
72	  const st = w.st;
73	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
74	  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % blockW / 18);
75	  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % blockH / 18);
76	  let d = delta;
77	  const ai = st.idx(ox, oy);
78	  if (axis === 'y' ? st.frameY[ai] >= threshold : st.frameX[ai] >= threshold) d = -delta;
79	  for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + tilesW; ix++) {
80	    for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + tilesH; iy++) {
81	      if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
82	      const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
83	      w.skipCell(ix, iy);
84	      if (axis === 'y') st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii] + d);
85	      else st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii] + d, st.frameY[ii]);
86	    }
87	  }
88	}

96	  if (st.frameX[i] >= delta) d = -delta;

157	export function switchMB(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number): void {
158	  const st = w.st;
159	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
160	  let num3 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18);
161	  while (num3 >= 2) num3 -= 2;

172	      st.setTile(k, l, st.type[ii], fx, st.frameY[ii]);
173	    }
174	  }
175	  w.skipCell(ox, oy); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy);
176	  w.skipCell(ox, oy + 1); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy + 1);
177	}

233	/** 陷阱(137)发射(Wiring.cs:1495-1743 + Item.cs placeStyle)。frameY/18=种类:
234	 *  0飞镖(98) 1超级飞镖(184) 2烈焰(187,隐形+尘埃) 3尖刺球(185) 4长矛(186) */
235	function fireTrap(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
236	  const st = w.st;
237	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
238	  const kind = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18);
239	  if (kind === 0 || kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
240	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 200)) return;
241	    // 方向:frameX 0=左 18=右;frameY<36 平置 / ≥36 竖置(上下)
242	    const dirX = st.frameX[i] === 0 ? -1 : st.frameX[i] === 18 ? 1 : 0;
243	    const dirY = st.frameX[i] < 36 ? 0 : st.frameX[i] < 72 ? -1 : 1;
244	    const speed = kind === 0 ? 12 : kind === 1 ? 12 : 5;
245	    const dmg = kind === 0 ? 20 : 40;
246	    const px = x * 16 + 8 + 10 * dirX, py = y * 16 + 8 + 10 * dirY;
247	    ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, dirX * speed, dirY * speed, dmg, kind === 0 ? 'dart' : kind === 1 ? 'superdart' : 'flame');
248	  } else if (kind === 3) {
249	    // 尖刺球:CheckMech 300 + 同场数量惩罚表(Wiring.cs:1536-1599)
250	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 300)) return;
251	    let budget = 200;
252	    const near = ctx.countProjectiles('spiky', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8, 1200);
253	    for (let k = 0; k < near; k++) {
254	      budget -= k < 1 ? 50 : k < 2 ? 15 : k < 4 ? 10 : k < 7 ? 8 : k < 12 ? 6 : k < 20 ? 5 : k < 38 ? 4 : k < 65 ? 3 : k < 105 ? 2 : 1;
255	    }
256	    if (budget <= 0) return;
257	    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
258	    switch (Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18)) {
259	      case 0: case 1: dy = 1; break;
260	      case 2: dy = -1; break;
261	      case 3: dx = -1; break;
262	      case 4: dx = 1; break;
263	    }
264	    const px = x * 16 + 8 + 14 * dx, py = y * 16 + 8 + 14 * dy;
265	    ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, dx * 4, dy * 4, 40, 'spiky');
266	  } else if (kind === 4) {
267	    // 下向火焰(CheckMech 90)
268	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 90)) return;
269	    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
270	    switch (Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18)) {
271	      case 0: dx = -1; break;
272	      case 1: dx = 1; break;
273	      case 2: dy = -1; break;
274	      case 3: dy = 1; break;
275	    }
276	    ctx.shootProjectile(x * 16 + 8 + 10 * dx, y * 16 + 8 + 10 * dy, dx * 8, dy * 8, 60, 'spear');
277	  }
278	}
279	
280	/** 热喷泉 443(Wiring.cs:2284-2312;由 HitSwitch 直调) */
281	export function geyserTrap(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): boolean {
282	  const st = w.st;
283	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
284	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
285	  const num = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 36);
286	  const i1 = x - Math.floor((st.frameX[i] - num * 36) / 18);
287	  if (!w.checkMech(i1, y, 200)) return false;
288	  const up = num < 2;
289	  ctx.shootProjectile(
290	    (i1 + 1) * 16, (y + (up ? 0 : 1)) * 16,
291	    0, up ? -8 : 8, 20, 'geyser',
292	  );
293	  return true;
294	}
295	
296	/** 雕像 style → 原版 NPC id 表(Wiring.cs:1892-1996)。
297	 *  num79 = frameX/36 + (frameY/54 % 3) * 55 */
298	const STATUE_NPC: Record<number, number | number[]> = {
299	  5: 73, 13: 24, 30: 6, 35: 2,
300	  51: [299, 538], 52: 356, 53: 357, 54: [355, 358], 55: [367, 366],
301	  56: [359, 359, 359, 359, 360], 57: 377, 58: 300, 59: [364, 362], 60: 148, 61: 361,
302	  62: [487, 486, 485], 64: 86, 65: 490, 66: 82, 67: 449, 68: 167, 69: 480, 70: 48,
303	  71: [170, 180, 171], 72: 481, 73: 482, 74: 430, 75: 489, 76: 611, 77: 602,
304	  78: [595, 596, 599, 597, 600, 598], 79: [616, 617],
305	  80: [671, 672], 81: 673, 82: [674, 675], // 1.4.4+ 新雕像（:2260-2267）
306	};
307	/** flag7（生成区实心 → poof 不生成）的 style（:2270-2282；64=小丑? 71=史莱姆王组） */
308	const STATUE_SOLID_CHECK = new Set([64, 71]);
309	/** 单独分支的雕像 style（Wiring.cs :2290-2440）。dx/dy 为生成点偏移、
310	 *  solid=生成区实心时 poof、pool=随机池（28 蛇三色等） */
311	const STATUE_NPC_DIRECT: Record<number, { npc: number | number[]; dx?: number; dy?: number; solid?: boolean }> = {
312	  4: { npc: 1, dy: -12 },
313	  7: { npc: 49, dx: -4, dy: -6 },
314	  8: { npc: 55, dy: -12 },
315	  9: { npc: 46, dy: -12 },
316	  10: { npc: 21 },
317	  16: { npc: 42, dy: -12, solid: true },
318	  18: { npc: 67, dy: -12 },
319	  23: { npc: 63, dy: -12 },
320	  27: { npc: 85, dx: -9 },
321	  28: { npc: [74, 297, 298], dy: -12 },
322	  42: { npc: 58, dy: -12 },
323	  50: { npc: 65, solid: true }, // 史莱姆王雕像（:2385-2399）
324	};
325	/** 物品雕像 style → [原版 item id, ...MechSpawn 配额同查的等价 id]
326	 *  （2 星 184/1735/1868、17 炸弹 166、37 心 58/1734/1867；:2408-2420） */
327	const STATUE_ITEM: Record<number, { item: number; mechIds: number[] }> = {
328	  2: { item: 184, mechIds: [184, 1735, 1868] },
329	  17: { item: 166, mechIds: [166] },
330	  37: { item: 58, mechIds: [58, 1734, 1867] },
331	};
332	
333	function pick<T>(arr: T[]): T { return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]; }
334	
335	/** 雕像 105(Wiring.cs:1866-2215) */
336	function fireStatue(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
337	  const st = w.st;
338	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
339	  // 锚点与 style(:1867-1879)
340	  const num73 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
341	  let num74 = 0;
342	  let rem = num73;
343	  while (rem >= 2) { rem -= 2; num74++; }
344	  const ox = x - rem;
345	  const ax = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
346	  const ay = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
347	  const num79 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 36) + Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 54 % 3) * 55;
348	  w.skipCell(ax, ay); w.skipCell(ax, ay + 1); w.skipCell(ax, ay + 2);
349	  w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay); w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay + 1); w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay + 2);
350	  const px = ax * 16 + 16, py = (ay + 3) * 16;
351	  // 生成区实心判定（:2274 SolidTiles(num139-2..num139+3, num138..num138+2)；flag7/部分直刷用）
352	  const spawnAreaSolid = () => {
353	    for (let sx = ax - 2; sx <= ax + 3; sx++) {
354	      for (let sy = ay; sy <= ay + 2; sy++) {
355	        if (st.inBounds(sx, sy) && st.isSolid(sx, sy)) return true;
356	      }
357	    }
358	    return false;
359	  };
360	  // NPC 雕像主表（spawn 于雕像底中心 (px,py)；:2268-2282）
361	  const entry = STATUE_NPC[num79];
362	  if (entry !== undefined) {
363	    const npcId = Array.isArray(entry) ? pick(entry) : entry;
364	    if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, npcId)) {
365	      if (!STATUE_SOLID_CHECK.has(num79) || !spawnAreaSolid()) {
366	        ctx.spawnNpc(npcId, px, py);
367	      } else {
368	        ctx.sfx('tink', px, py);
369	      }
370	    }
371	    return;
372	  }
373	  // 单独分支 NPC 雕像（各自偏移/实心判定；:2290-2440）
374	  const direct = STATUE_NPC_DIRECT[num79];
375	  if (direct !== undefined) {
376	    const npcId = Array.isArray(direct.npc) ? pick(direct.npc) : direct.npc;
377	    if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, npcId)) {
378	      if (!direct.solid || !spawnAreaSolid()) {
379	        ctx.spawnNpc(npcId, px + (direct.dx ?? 0), py + (direct.dy ?? 0));
380	      } else {
381	        ctx.sfx('tink', px, py);
382	      }
383	    }
384	    return;
385	  }
386	  // 物品雕像（CheckMech 600；心/星要过全部等价 id 的 MechSpawn 配额；:2408-2420）
387	  const itemEntry = STATUE_ITEM[num79];
388	  if (itemEntry !== undefined) {
389	    const ok = w.checkMech(ax, ay, 600)
390	      && itemEntry.mechIds.every((mid) => ctx.itemSpawnOk(px, py, mid));
391	    if (ok) ctx.dropItem(itemEntry.item, px, py - 16);
392	    return;
393	  }
394	  switch (num79) {
395	    case 34: { // 蘑菇雕像:2×3 变 tile 349 动画(:2108-2119)
396	      const T349 = TILE_BY_KEY['v_349_mushroom_statue'] ?? 0;
397	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
398	        for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
399	          st.setTile(ax + dx, ay + dy, T349, dx * 18 + 216, dy * 18);
400	        }
401	      }
402	      return;
403	    }
404	    case 40: case 41: // 城镇 NPC 搬迁(CheckMech 300,:2133-2186)
405	      if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 300)) ctx.relocateTownNpc(num79 === 40 ? 0 : 1, px, py);
406	      return;
407	    case 63: // 随机传送雕像：164 + 额外 MechSpawn(165)（:2226-2229）
408	      if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, 164) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, 165)) {
409	        ctx.spawnNpc(164, px, py);
410	      }
411	      return;
412	  }
413	}

415	/** 巨石雕像 531(Wiring.cs:1747-1762) */
416	function fireBoulderStatue(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
417	  const st = w.st;
418	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
419	  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
420	  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 36 / 18);
421	  if (!w.checkMech(ox, oy, 900)) return;
422	  w.skipCell(ox, oy); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy); w.skipCell(ox, oy + 1); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy + 1);
423	  ctx.shootProjectile(ox * 16 + 16, (oy + 1) * 16, 0, 0, 70, 'boulder');
424	}

426	/** 可接线灯器件的开关帧判定(Wiring.cs 翻帧 delta 推导):on = frame%(2δ) < δ。
427	 *  火把 δ=66(22px×3 变体一组)、蜡烛族 δ=18、2×2 灯具 δ=36、吊灯/彩灯 δ=54、篝火走 Y 轴 δ=36 */
428	export const LIGHT_TOGGLE: Record<number, ['x' | 'y', number]> = {
429	  4: ['x', 66],
430	  33: ['x', 18], 174: ['x', 18], 49: ['x', 18], 372: ['x', 18],
431	  42: ['x', 18], 92: ['x', 18], 93: ['x', 18],
432	  95: ['x', 36], 100: ['x', 36], 126: ['x', 36], 173: ['x', 36], 564: ['x', 36],
433	  34: ['x', 54], 149: ['x', 54],
434	  215: ['y', 36],
435	};
436	
437	/** 该格灯光是否点亮(非灯器件恒 true;供 LightingEngine 查询) */
438	export function lightIsOn(sheet: number, frameX: number, frameY: number): boolean {
439	  const t = LIGHT_TOGGLE[sheet];
440	  if (!t) return true;
441	  const v = t[0] === 'x' ? frameX : frameY;
442	  return v % (2 * t[1]) < t[1];
443	}
444	
445	/** HitWireSingle 器件主分发表。返回 true = 已处理 */
446	export function deviceTable(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, sheet: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
447	  const st = w.st;
448	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
449	  switch (sheet) {
450	    case 4: // 火把 frameX ±66(:1207-1213)
451	      flipCell(w, x, y, 66);
452	      return true;
453	    case 10: // 关门 → 开(随机方向+反向,原版 :1171-1188)
454	      ctx.doorOpen(x, y);
455	      return true;
456	    case 11:
457	      ctx.doorClose(x, y);
458	      return true;
459	    case 15: { // 马桶(frameY/40 判定,:1194-1204;喷水近似粒子)
460	      if (Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 1 && Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 20) return true;
461	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 60)) ctx.sfx('splash', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
462	      return true;
463	    }
464	    case 33: case 174: case 49: case 372: // 蜡烛族 frameX ±18(:1472-1480)
465	      flipCell(w, x, y, 18);
466	      return true;
467	    case 34: // 吊灯 3×3 frameX ±54(:1405-1428)
468	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 108, 54, 3, 3, 'x', 54, 54);
469	      return true;
470	    case 35: case 139: // 南瓜灯/八音盒：SwitchMB（WorldGen.cs:50977-51021；Wiring.cs:2019-2022）
471	      switchMB(w, x, y);
472	      return true;
473	    case 207: // 喷泉：SwitchFountain（WorldGen.cs:51171-51209；Wiring.cs:2023）
474	      switchFountain(w, x, y);
475	      return true;
476	    case 410: case 480: case 509: case 657: case 658: case 720: case 721: case 725: case 733:
477	      // 天塔柱族：SwitchMonolith（WorldGen.cs:51023-51169；Wiring.cs:2032-2036）
478	      switchMonolith(w, x, y);
479	      return true;
480	    case 42: // 挂链灯笼 1×2 frameX ±18(:1214-1227)
481	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 36, 36, 1, 2, 'x', 18, 18);
482	      return true;
483	    case 92: // 路灯柱 1×6 frameX ±18(:1483-1494)
484	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 108, 1, 6, 'x', 18, 18);
485	      return true;
486	    case 93: // 蒂基火炬 1×3 frameX ±18(:1228-1243)
487	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 54, 1, 3, 'x', 18, 18);
488	      return true;
489	    case 95: case 100: case 126: case 173: case 564: // 2×2 灯具族 frameX ±36(:1381-1404)
490	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
491	      return true;
492	    case 105:
493	      fireStatue(w, ctx, x, y);
494	      return true;
495	    case 130: { // 通电石:上方有宝箱则不缩(:878-884)
496	      const above = y > 0 ? w.sheetOf(x, y - 1) : -1;
497	      if (above === 21 || above === 88 || above === 467) return true;
498	      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
499	      return true;
500	    }
501	    case 131:
502	      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
503	      return true;
504	    case 137:
505	      fireTrap(w, ctx, x, y);
506	      return true;
507	    case 141: // 炸药(:1781-1785)：清格 + Projectile 108(500伤/半径10)
508	      w.skipCell(x, y);
509	      st.setTile(x, y, 0);
510	      ctx.explode(x, y, 500, 10);
511	      return true;
512	    case 149: // 节日彩灯 frameX ±54(:1244-1250)
513	      flipCell(w, x, y, 54);
514	      return true;
515	    case 219:
516	    case 642: { // 提取机（Wiring.cs:1993-1995 → Extractinator :2563-2593）：
517	      // 通电自动消耗邻域箱子里的可提取物。锚点按 3×3(54px) 帧内偏移回算（:2567-2568），
518	      // 冷却 checkMech 60t（:2569/:2571）；箱子查找/消耗/掉落在 ctx（Game 侧拥有箱子与物品栏）。
519	      const ax = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 54 / 18);
520	      const ay = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
521	      if (!w.checkMech(ax, ay, 60)) return true;
522	      const out = ctx.extractinatorPull(ax, ay, sheet);
523	      if (out && out.itemType > 0) {
524	        // :2588 Item.NewItem(num*16, num2*16, 32, 32) → 锚点 2×2 判定框中心
525	        ctx.dropItemStack(out.itemType, out.stack, ax * 16 + 16, ay * 16 + 16);
526	      }
527	      return true;
528	    }
529	    case 209: { // 大炮（Wiring.cs:1237-1346 1:1）：旋转+开火复合状态机。
530	      // num31=块内列(0/3=旋转态,1/2=待发) num32=行内偏移 num35=仰角带(frameY/54,0-8)
531	      // num36=水平带(frameX/72: 0/1=右向 大炮/兔兔炮, 3/4=左向)
532	      const num31 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 72 / 18);
533	      const num32 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
534	      const ox = x - num31, oy = y - num32;
535	      const num35 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 54);
536	      const num36 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 72);
537	      let num37 = -1;
538	      if (num31 === 1 || num31 === 2) num37 = num32;
539	      // 旋转半边：列3 → 仰角 -54（带0停）；列0 → +54（带8停）
540	      let num38 = 0;
541	      if (num31 === 3) num38 = -54;
542	      if (num31 === 0) num38 = 54;
543	      if (num35 >= 8 && num38 > 0) num38 = 0;
544	      if (num35 === 0 && num38 < 0) num38 = 0;
545	      if (num38 !== 0) {
546	        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
547	          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
548	            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
549	            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
550	            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
551	            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci], st.frameY[ci] + num38);
552	          }
553	        }
554	      }
555	      // 水平朝向带翻转：3/4 带 + 行0/1 → frameX ±72（下一次触发反向）
556	      if ((num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) && (num37 === 0 || num37 === 1)) {
557	        const fx = num36 === 3 ? 72 : -72;
558	        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
559	          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
560	            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
561	            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
562	            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
563	            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci] + fx, st.frameY[ci]);
564	          }
565	        }
566	      }
567	      if (num37 === -1) return true; // 只旋转未开火
568	      // 开火半边：半仰角(<2)时左向(带3/4)不发射（flag2，Wiring.cs:1301-1304）
569	      const flag2 = !((num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) && num37 < 2);
570	      // 伤害/CheckMech 时长（Wiring.cs:1306-1330）：0=大炮 300/480，1=兔兔炮 350/3600，
571	      // 其余（3/4 传送门炮）damage 0 / time 30——原版 switch 只设这两档
572	      const damage = num36 === 0 ? 300 : num36 === 1 ? 350 : 0;
573	      const time = num36 === 0 ? 480 : num36 === 1 ? 3600 : 30;
574	      // 全局冷却门（Wiring.cs:1312-1314/1321-1323：冷却中直接返回）
575	      if (num36 === 0 && w.cannonCoolDown > 0) return true;
576	      if (num36 === 1 && w.bunnyCannonCoolDown > 0) return true;
577	      if (!flag2 || !w.checkMech(ox, oy, time)) return true;
578	      // WorldGen.ShootFromCannon（WorldGen.cs:50606-50718 1:1）
579	      // 弹种（:50611-50631）：ammo=num36+1 → 0→162 炮弹 / 1→281 爆炸兔兔 / 3·4→601（速度 3）
580	      const speed = num36 === 3 || num36 === 4 ? 3 : 14; // :50608 num=14f；601 时 num=3f(:50623/50628)
581	      // 初速方向表（:50640-50683）：仰角带 0-8 → (10,0)…(0,-10)…(-10,0)
582	      const ANGLE_V: Array<readonly [number, number]> = [
583	        [10, 0], [7.5, -2.5], [5, -5], [2.75, -6], [0, -10],
584	        [-2.75, -6], [-5, -5], [-7.5, -2.5], [-10, 0],
585	      ];
586	      const [bx, by] = ANGLE_V[num35] ?? [0, -10];
587	      // 归一化到 speed（:50706-50708 num8 = num/|v|）
588	      const len = Math.hypot(bx, by);
589	      const vx = (bx * speed) / len, vy = (by * speed) / len;
590	      // 出膛位置（:50685 (x+2)*16,(y+2)*16；601 仅加 (0/5,5) 偏移 :50689-50696）
591	      let px = (ox + 2) * 16, py = (oy + 2) * 16;
592	      if (num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) {
593	        if (num35 === 4) px += 5;
594	        py += 5;
595	      }
596	      // num36 3/4 原版发 PortalGunBolt 601（WorldGen.cs:50622-50631）：速度 3、damage 0、
597	      // ai[0] = ammo5 ? 1 : 0（传送门 form，橙 0/蓝 1）。撞块 → PortalHelper.TryPlacingPortal
598	      // 落门（entities/Portal.ts），不再沿用巨石弹体
599	      const kind: TrapShotKind = num36 === 0 ? 'cannonball' : num36 === 1 ? 'bunny' : 'portalbolt';
600	      const form = num36 === 4 ? 1 : 0; // :50622-50631（ammo4 → ai0=0 / ammo5 → ai0=1）
601	      // 全局冷却置位（Wiring.cs:1334-1339：大炮 120 / 兔兔炮 480）——先于弹内判定，
602	      // 与原版一致（冷却在 HitWireSingle 置位，BunnyCannonCanFire 在 ShootFromCannon 内）
603	      if (num36 === 0) w.cannonCoolDown = 120;
604	      if (num36 === 1) w.bunnyCannonCoolDown = 480;
605	      // 兔兔炮场内上限（WorldGen.cs:50697-50701 → BunnyCannonCanFire :50722-50760：
606	      //   NPC614+弹281 计数 ≥4 拒绝；NPC614 无对应实体，此处只数 281 弹）
607	      if (kind === 'bunny' && ctx.countProjectiles('bunny', px, py, Infinity) >= 4) return true;
608	      ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind, form);
609	      return true;
610	    }
611	    case 210: // 地雷 ExplodeMine(:3093)：清格 + Projectile 164(250伤)——
612	      // 164 不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表内 = **不破坏地形**（纯伤害爆炸）
613	      st.setTile(x, y, 0);
614	      ctx.explode(x, y, 250, 0, false);
615	      return true;
616	    case 212: { // 雪球发射器（Wiring.cs:1345-1418 1:1）：旋转+开火复合状态机。
617	      // num51=块内列(0/2=旋转态,1=待发) num52=行内偏移 num55=朝向带(frameX/54,0左 1右)
618	      const num51 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 54 / 18);
619	      const num52 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
620	      const ox = x - num51, oy = y - num52;
621	      const num55 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 54);
622	      let num56 = -1;
623	      if (num51 === 1) num56 = num52;
624	      // 瞄准旋转：列0→frameX -54（带0停）、列2→+54（带1停）（:1359-1365）
625	      let num57 = 0;
626	      if (num51 === 0) num57 = -54;
627	      if (num51 === 2) num57 = 54;
628	      if (num55 >= 1 && num57 > 0) num57 = 0;
629	      if (num55 === 0 && num57 < 0) num57 = 0;
630	      if (num57 !== 0) {
631	        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 3; cx++) {
632	          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
633	            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
634	            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
635	            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
636	            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci] + num57, st.frameY[ci]);
637	          }
638	        }
639	      }
640	      if (num56 === -1) return true; // 只旋转未开火
641	      // 开火门（:1391）：全局冷却 0 + CheckMech(锚点,60)
642	      if (w.snowballCannonCoolDown > 0 || !w.checkMech(ox, oy, 60)) return true;
643	      w.snowballCannonCoolDown = 15; // :1393
644	      // 弹速（:1394）：12 + rand(0..449)*0.01 = 12.00..16.49
645	      const speed = 12 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 450) * 0.01;
646	      // 方向（:1395-1396）：X=rand(85..104) / Y=rand(-35..10)（Main.rand.Next 上界开区间）
647	      let dx = 85 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
648	      const dy = -35 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 46);
649	      // 出膛位置（:1400 (x+2)*16-8,(y+2)*16-8）+ 朝向偏移（:1401-1405：左带 X-12 / 右带 X+12）
650	      let px = (ox + 2) * 16 - 8, py = (oy + 2) * 16 - 8;
651	      if (num55 === 0) { dx *= -1; px -= 12; } else { px += 12; }
652	      // 归一化到 speed（:1410-1415）后发射 166 雪球：35 伤 / 3.5 击退（:1397-1399）
653	      const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
654	      ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, (dx * speed) / len, (dy * speed) / len, 35, 'snowball');
655	      return true;
656	    }
657	    case 215: // 篝火 3×2 frameY ±36(:1003-1020)
658	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 36, 3, 2, 'y', 36, 36);
659	      return true;
660	    case 216: // 烟花火箭(:1189-1192;近似粒子升空)
661	      w.skipCell(x, y);
662	      ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
663	      return true;
664	    case 244: // 泡泡机 3×2 frameX ±54(:1272-1293)
665	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 36, 3, 2, 'x', 54, 54);
666	      return true;
667	    case 255: case 256: case 257: case 258: case 259: case 260: case 261: { // 宝石块 off→on(:863-873)
668	      if (st.wire[i] & 16) return true; // 原版有致动器时不响应
669	      const to = TILE_BY_KEY[GEMSPARK_ON[sheet - 255]] ?? 0;
670	      if (to) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
671	      return true;
672	    }
673	    case 262: case 263: case 264: case 265: case 266: case 267: case 268: { // on→off
674	      if (st.wire[i] & 16) return true;
675	      const to = TILE_BY_KEY[GEMSPARK_OFF[sheet - 262]] ?? 0;
676	      if (to) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
677	      return true;
678	    }
679	    case 314: // 压板轨道（Wiring.cs:1429-1433）：CheckMech 5 + FlipSwitchTrack 翻转轨道指向。
680	      // 【依赖登记】FlipSwitchTrack 依赖矿车轨道系统（轨道帧族 314 的箭头帧语义），
681	      // 矿车系统接入前只走冷却注册，不翻帧
682	      w.checkMech(x, y, 5);
683	      return true;
684	    case 335: // 烟花盒 2×2(:1294-1304)
685	      w.skipCell(x, y); w.skipCell(x + 1, y);
686	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 30)) ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
687	      return true;
688	    case 338: // 烟花喷泉 1×2(:1305-1324)
689	      w.skipCell(x, y);
690	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 30)) ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
691	      return true;
692	    case 386: case 387: { // 活板门开/关互换(ShiftTrapdoor;帧近似)
693	      const openK = 'v_386_trapdooropen', closedK = 'v_387_trap_door_closed';
694	      const toOpen = sheet === 387;
695	      const target = TILE_BY_KEY[toOpen ? openK : closedK] ?? 0;
696	      if (!target) return true;
697	      const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
698	      for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + 2; ix++) {
699	        if (!st.inBounds(ix, y)) continue;
700	        const ii = st.idx(ix, y);
701	        w.skipCell(ix, y);
702	        st.setTile(ix, y, target, (ix - ox) * 18, st.frameY[ii]);
703	      }
704	      return true;
705	    }
706	    case 388: case 389: { // 高门关/开互换(ShiftTallGate;帧近似)
707	      const openK = 'v_389_tallgateopen', closedK = 'v_388_tall_gate_closed';
708	      const toOpen = sheet === 388;
709	      const target = TILE_BY_KEY[toOpen ? openK : closedK] ?? 0;
710	      if (!target) return true;
711	      const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 20 / 18);
712	      const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 90 / 18);
713	      for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + 1; ix++) {
714	        for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + 5; iy++) {
715	          if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
716	          const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
717	          w.skipCell(ix, iy);
718	          st.setTile(ix, iy, target, st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii]);
719	        }
720	      }
721	      return true;
722	    }
723	    case 405: // 壁炉 3×2 frameX ±54(:1021-1038)
724	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 36, 3, 2, 'x', 54, 54);
725	      return true;
726	    case 406: // 烟囱 3×3 frameY +54/-108(:1039-1056)
727	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 54, 3, 3, 'y', 108, 54);
728	      return true;
729	    case 411: // 引爆器 2×2 frameX ±36(:1057-1074)
730	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 36, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
731	      return true;
732	    case 421: case 422: { // 传送带反向互换(:844-861;有致动器不响应)
733	      if (st.wire[i] & 16) return true;
734	      const other = TILE_BY_KEY[sheet === 421 ? 'v_422_conveyor_belt_ccw' : 'v_421_conveyorbeltleft'] ?? 0;
735	      if (other) st.setTile(x, y, other, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
736	      return true;
737	    }
738	    case 425: { // 广播盒（Wiring.cs:1177-1230 1:1）：锚点 2×2 内登记的告示牌文本 →
739	      // 粉色聊天广播（NewTextMultiline）；无牌/空文本静默返回。
740	      // AnnouncementBoxRange 默认 -1 = 全图（设置项，引擎暂未接设置门，恒全图）。
741	      // 【依赖登记】牌面文字编辑 UI 未接入（现存 signs 只读：墓碑/导入存档）
742	      const num5 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
743	      const num6 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 36 / 18);
744	      const ox = x - num5, oy = y - num6;
745	      for (let m = ox; m < ox + 2; m++) {
746	        for (let n = oy; n < oy + 2; n++) {
747	          if (st.inBounds(m, n)) w.skipCell(m, n);
748	        }
749	      }
750	      ctx.announce?.(ox, oy);
751	      return true;
752	    }
753	    case 442: return true; // 投射物垫板(触发源侧处理)
754	    case 443:
755	      geyserTrap(w, ctx, x, y);
756	      return true;
757	    case 452: // 呆萌气球机 3×3 frameX ±54(:1132-1149)
758	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 54, 3, 3, 'x', 54, 54);
759	      return true;
760	    case 455: // 派对纪念碑（Wiring.cs:2037-2039 → BirthdayParty.ToggleManualParty，
761	      // BirthdayParty.cs:145-163）：切换 ManualParty（开→升气球天/彩带，入夜 CheckNight 结束）
762	      ctx.toggleParty?.();
763	      return true;
764	    case 476: return true; // 高尔夫球洞(无高尔夫系统)
765	    case 497: // 马桶(frameY/40,同 case 15 分支)
766	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 60)) ctx.sfx('splash', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
767	      return true;
768	    case 506: // 巴斯特雕像 2×3 frameX ±72(:2239-2261)
769	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 54, 2, 3, 'x', 72, 72);
770	      return true;
771	    case 531:
772	      fireBoulderStatue(w, ctx, x, y);
773	      return true;
774	    case 546: case 557: // 格栅开/关互换(:2262-2271)

776	      return true;
777	    case 565: // 造雾机 2×2 frameX ±36(:1358-1379)
778	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
779	      return true;
780	    case 593: // 小火山 frameX ±18(:1434-1445;动画近似粒子)

783	      return true;
784	    case 594: // 大火山 2×2 frameX ±36(:1446-1470)
785	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
786	      ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);

788	  }
789	  return false;
790	}

792	/** 装配:把器件表挂到 Wiring(器件 case 之外的默认落点) */
793	export function attachDevices(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx): void {
794	  w.device = (ww, sheet, x, y) => deviceTable(ww, ctx, sheet, x, y);
795	  w.teleportPair = (a, b) => ctx.teleportPair(a, b);
796	}

93	  /** 全透明贴图缓存(xnb 解包异常的 187/654 等):检出一次后走色块回退,避免隐形弹幕 */
94	  private static blankTex = new Set<string>();
95	  private static okTex = new Set<string>();
96	  private static isBlank(r: Renderer, path: string): boolean {
97	    if (TrapShot.blankTex.has(path)) return true;
98	    if (TrapShot.okTex.has(path)) return false;
99	    const img = r.atlas?.vimages.get(path);
100	    let blank = true;
101	    if (img) {
102	      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
103	      c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
104	      const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
105	      cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
106	      const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
107	      for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) { if (d[i] > 30) { blank = false; break; } }
108	    }
109	    (blank ? TrapShot.blankTex : TrapShot.okTex).add(path);
110	    return blank;
111	  }
112	
113	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: TrapShotKind, form = 0) {
114	    super();
115	    const s = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[kind];
116	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
117	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
118	    this.damage = damage;
119	    this.kind = kind;
120	    this.form = form;
121	    this.w = s.w; this.h = s.h;
122	    if (kind === 'spiky') this.life = 1800; // 原版尖刺球 timeLeft 1800
123	    if (s.life !== undefined) this.life = s.life; // 炮弹族 timeLeft（SetDefaults 默认 3600 / 281=600）
124	  }
125	
126	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
127	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
128	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
129	    if (!ctx) return;
130	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
131	    const img = st.proj && r.atlas ? r.atlas.vimages.get(st.proj) : null;
132	    if (img && st.fw && st.fh && !TrapShot.isBlank(r, st.proj!)) {
133	      // 原版弹幕贴图(整帧):飞行类贴图默认朝上(镖尖/火苗向上),对齐速度方向需 +90°;
134	      // 尖刺球/巨石不随速度旋转,按位移滚转
135	      ctx.save();
136	      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
137	      if (st.spin) ctx.rotate(this.rot);
138	      else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (st.rotOff ?? 0));
139	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, st.fw, st.fh, -st.fw / 2, -st.fh / 2, st.fw, st.fh);
140	      ctx.restore();
141	      return;
142	    }
143	    ctx.fillStyle = st.color;
144	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
145	  }
146	
147	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
148	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
149	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
150	    if (--this.life <= 0) {
151	      // 炮弹族 timeLeft 耗尽同样走 Kill 爆炸（Projectile.Kill 由 timeLeft 归零触发）
152	      if (TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind].blast) this.blastKill(game);
153	      else this.dead = true;
154	      return;
155	    }
156	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
157	    this.age++;
158	    // 首帧发射音（Projectile.AI：162=Projectile.cs:21542-21546 / 281=26830-26833 SoundID.Item14）
159	    if (this.age === 1 && st.launchSfx) game.playSfx(st.launchSfx, 1, this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
160	    // 滚转：尖刺球/巨石近似 | 炮弹族按原版
161	    if (st.spin === true) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;
162	    else if (st.spin === 'roll') {
163	      // aiStyle2（Projectile.cs:21528）：rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*direction
164	      this.rot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1);
165	    } else if (st.spin === 'bunny') {
166	      // aiStyle49（Projectile.cs:26841）：rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05
167	      this.rot += this.vx * 0.05 + (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.05;
168	    }
169	    if (st.gravDelay !== undefined) {
170	      // 炮弹族弧线：前 gravDelay tick 直线，之后 vy+=grav / vx*=xDamp
171	      // （162/281=Projectile.cs:21546-21550/26844-26849 的 ai[0]>=18；166=:21879-21884 的 >=20）
172	      if (this.age >= st.gravDelay) {
173	        this.vy += st.grav;
174	        this.vx *= st.xDamp ?? 1;
175	        if (st.vCap !== undefined && this.vy > st.vCap) this.vy = st.vCap; // 281 :26850-26853
176	      }
177	    } else {
178	      this.vy += st.grav;
179	    }
180	    this.x += this.vx;
181	    this.y += this.vy;
182	    // 粒子拖尾(原版火焰陷阱/热喷泉的视觉主体是沿途粒子;每 2 tick 一次防刷屏)
183	    if (st.trail && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
184	      game.spawnParticles(
185	        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
186	        st.trail.colors[(this.life >> 1) % st.trail.colors.length],
187	        st.trail.count, 0.4, { life: st.trail.life, damp: 0.96, grav: st.trail.grav },
188	      );
189	    }
190	    // 传送门弹拖尾（Projectile.cs:51180-51192：尘 264 = 门色，无重力）
191	    if (this.kind === 'portalbolt' && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
192	      game.spawnParticles(
193	        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
194	        this.form === 0 ? '#FFAA28' : '#3CE0FF', 1, 0.6, { life: 14, damp: 0.94, grav: 0 },
195	      );
196	    }
197	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
198	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
199	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
200	      // 传送门弹撞块（Projectile.cs:16672-16686）：落门后消亡（音效 Item115/114 按 form）
201	      if (this.kind === 'portalbolt') {
202	        game.placePortal?.(this.x, this.y, this.vx, this.vy, this.form);
203	        game.playSfx(this.form === 0 ? 'portalOrange' : 'portalBlue', 1, this.x, this.y);
204	        this.dead = true;
205	        return;
206	      }
207	      // 尖刺球/巨石:撞块反弹衰减
208	      if (this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'boulder') {
209	        if (Math.abs(this.vy) > 1) { this.vy *= -0.4; this.vx *= 0.7; this.y -= this.vy; }
210	        else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.94; }
211	      } else if (st.blast) {
212	        // 炮弹/兔兔炮撞块 → Kill 落点爆炸（Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / 72704-72758）
213	        this.blastKill(game);
214	        return;
215	      } else {
216	        // 雪球撞块碎裂（Projectile.cs:71758-71767）
217	        if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);
218	        this.dead = true;
219	        return;
220	      }
221	    }
222	    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）：不伤玩家，直接进敌怪判定。
223	    // 敌对陷阱：伤害走 damagePlayer(防御减算/受击音/iframes 去重);除巨石外命中即碎
224	    if (!st.friendly) {
225	      if (projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, game.player) && !game.player.dead) {
226	        hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);
227	        if (this.kind !== 'boulder') this.dead = true;
228	        if (this.dead) return;
229	      }
230	    }
231	
232	    // ---- 命中怪物/小动物/城镇 NPC（原版陷阱弹幕对全实体敌对，Projectile.DamageNPC 语义） ----
233	    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；
234	    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）；
235	    // 炮弹族：162 penetrate4 / 281 penetrate-1（SetDefaults）→ 穿透，撞块/超时才 Kill 爆炸；
236	    // 166 雪球 penetrate1 → 一碰即碎
237	    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame'
238	      || this.kind === 'cannonball' || this.kind === 'bunny';
239	    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
240	      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;
241	    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）
242	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
243	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
244	      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;
245	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
246	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
247	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
248	      }
249	      if (!pierce) {
250	        if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);
251	        this.dead = true;
252	        return;
253	      }
254	    }
255	    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）到此为止：不误伤小动物/城镇 NPC
256	    // （原版 friendly 弹对城镇 NPC 免疫；小动物不在敌怪判定内）
257	    if (st.friendly) return;
258	    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）
259	    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;
260	    if (buckets) {
261	      for (const c of buckets.critters) {
262	        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;
263	        c.hurt(game);
264	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
265	      }
266	      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）
267	      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {
268	        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;
269	        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
270	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
271	      }
272	    }
273	  }
274	
275	  /** 雪球碎裂（Projectile.Kill type166，Projectile.cs:71758-71767：尘 76 十粒 + Item51） */
276	  private shatter(game: GameHooks): void {
277	    game.spawnParticles(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, '#F2F8FF', 10, 1, { life: 20, damp: 0.94, grav: -0.01 });
278	    game.playSfx('shatter', 1, this.x, this.y);
279	  }
280	
281	  /** 落点爆炸（Projectile.Kill：type162=Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / type281=72704-72758）：
282	   *  纯范围伤害（162/281 均不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表 Projectile.cs:75260 → 不破坏地形）；
283	   *  原版两段伤害盒 162 为 64×64→144×144、281 为 128×128，此处按 blast 半宽合并一段；
284	   *  friendly 弹不伤玩家（hostile 伤害仅在 getGoodWorld 下对玩家生效，Main.getGoodWorld） */
285	  private blastKill(game: GameHooks): void {
286	    this.dead = true;
287	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
288	    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
289	    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy); // SoundID.Item14（Projectile.cs:72769/72706）
290	    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFA050', 14, 3.5, { life: 18, damp: 0.93, grav: -0.02 });
291	    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFF0C0', 8, 4.5, { life: 10, damp: 0.9, grav: -0.05 });
292	    const r = st.blast!;
293	    const box = { x: cx - r, y: cy - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r };
294	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
295	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
296	      if (e.dead) continue;
297	      if (!(box.x < e.x + e.w && box.x + box.w > e.x && box.y < e.y + e.h && box.y + box.h > e.y)) continue;
298	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
299	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
300	        game.addDamageNumber(cx, cy, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
301	      }
302	    }
303	  }
304	}

306	export class Dart extends Entity {
307	  w = 8; h = 4;
308	  vx: number;
309	  vy: number;
310	  damage: number;
311	  life = 600; // tick 上限(原版 timeLeft 600)
312	  dead = false;
313	
314	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number) {
315	    super();
316	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
317	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
318	    this.damage = damage;
319	  }
320	
321	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
322	    // 世界坐标绘制(同 TrapShot)
323	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
324	    if (!ctx) return;
325	    ctx.fillStyle = '#C8B89A';
326	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
327	  }
328	
329	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
330	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
331	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
332	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
333	    const world = game.world;
334	    this.x += this.vx;
335	    this.y += this.vy;
336	    // 撞实心块消失
337	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
338	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
339	    if (world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
340	    // 命中玩家(敌对陷阱):矩形相交即消耗弹体;伤害走 damagePlayer(防御减算/受击音/
341	    // iframes 去重——无敌帧内命中不扣血但弹体照常销毁,原版语义)
342	    if (projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, game.player) && !game.player.dead) {
343	      hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);
344	      this.dead = true;
345	    }
346	  }
347	}

349	/**
350	 * 629 TowerDamageBolt（Projectile.cs AI_122 :33747-33778 + Kill 段 :69784-69819）：
351	 * 本组教徒怪死亡时朝对应塔发射的红色追踪弹——ai[0]=目标塔 NPC，
352	 * 每 tick velocity = normalize(塔心-弹心)×5（:33775-33777）+ Dust.QuickDust 红尘尾，
353	 * 命中塔 Hitbox → Kill：盾 -1（Game 侧 onTowerBoltHit）且盾非 0 时塔 ai[3]=1（闪光 120t）。
354	 * 目标失联（塔死亡/消失）立即消亡（:33753-33761）。
355	 * 纯事件弹：无伤害（damage=0）、无碰撞、不参与联机弹幕同步（视觉弹） */
356	export class TowerBolt extends Entity {
357	  w = 10; h = 10;
358	  /** 目标塔解析（每 tick 重查——塔可能在本弹飞行中死亡） */
359	  private target: () => Enemy | null;
360	  /** 命中回调（盾结算/闪光/盾破音，Game 注入） */
361	  private onHit: (tower: Enemy) => void;
362	  private life = 3600;
363	
364	  constructor(x: number, y: number, target: () => Enemy | null, onHit: (tower: Enemy) => void) {
365	    super();
366	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
367	    this.target = target;
368	    this.onHit = onHit;
369	  }
370	
371	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
372	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
373	    if (!ctx) return;
374	    // 世界坐标绘制：红色发光点（原版贴图 Projectile_629 未解包时以粒子红点代）
375	    const img = r.atlas ? r.atlas.vimages.get('vanilla/Projectile_629.png') : null;
376	    if (img) {
377	      ctx.drawImage(img, this.x + this.w / 2 - img.width / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - img.height / 2);
378	      return;
379	    }
380	    ctx.fillStyle = '#FF4040';
381	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
382	    ctx.fillStyle = '#FFB0A0';
383	    ctx.fillRect(this.x + 3, this.y + 3, this.w - 6, this.h - 6);
384	  }
385	
386	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
387	    const t = this.target();
388	    if (!t || t.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }   // AI_122 目标失联即 Kill（:33753-33761）
389	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
390	    const dx = t.cx - this.cx, dy = t.cy - this.cy;
391	    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
392	    this.vx = (dx / len) * 5;                          // :33775 normalize(塔心-弹心)×5
393	    this.vy = (dy / len) * 5;
394	    this.x += this.vx;
395	    this.y += this.vy;
396	    // 红尘尾（:33778 Dust.QuickDust(Center, Red)——无重力短命尘）
397	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, Math.random() < 0.5 ? '#FF4040' : '#FF9060',
398	      1, 0.5, { life: 12, damp: 0.95, grav: 0 });
399	    // 命中塔 Hitbox（:33765-33770）→ Kill → 盾结算回调
400	    if (this.aabbOverlaps(t)) {
401	      this.dead = true;
402	      this.onHit(t);
403	    }
404	  }
405	}
406

  private checkTorchDetach(x: number, y: number) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const TORCH_ID = T.TORCH;
    for (const [dx, dy] of [[0, 0], [0, -1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]] as const) {
      const tx = x + dx, ty = y + dy;
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
      if (st.type[i] !== TORCH_ID) continue;
      if (torchStillAnchored(st, tx, ty, st.frameX[i])) continue;
      st.setTile(tx, ty, 0);
      this.spawnDrop(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE, 'torch', 1);
      this.sfx.play('dig');
    }
  }

  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed = 2,
    opts: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number } = {},
  ) {
    const base = opts.life ?? 40;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const life = Math.round(base * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.4));
      this.particles.push({
        x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed * Math.random(), vy: Math.sin(a) * speed * Math.random() - 1,
        life, maxLife: life, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 2,
        damp: opts.damp, grav: opts.grav,
      });
    }
    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);
  }

  setZoom(z: number) {
    if (!this.camera) return;
    this.camera.setZoomTarget(z);
  }

  // ================= 合成 =================

  itemId(key: string): number {
    return ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
  }

  stationsNearby(): Set<string> {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const px = Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE), py = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
    const found = new Set<string>(['hand']);
    for (let dx = -4; dx <= 4; dx++) {
      for (let dy = -3; dy <= 3; dy++) {
        const t = st.get(px + dx, py + dy);
        if (t === T.WORKBENCH) found.add('workbench');
        if (t === T.FURNACE) found.add('furnace');
        if (t === T.ANVIL) found.add('anvil');
      }

      },
      spawnNpc: (vanillaNpcId: number, px: number, py: number) => {
        const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(vanillaNpcId, px, py);
        if (e) {
          // Wiring.cs:2479-2483：雕像产怪 value=0（不掉钱）+ SpawnedFromStatue
          e.spawnedFromStatue = true;
          this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      },
      mechSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // NPC.MechSpawn（NPC.cs:7399-7483）：同 type（等价组互计）三阈值拒绝
        const grp = NPC_MECH_GROUPS.find((g) => g.includes(type));
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.enemies) {
          const en = o as Enemy;
          const t = en.vanillaId;
          if (t == null) continue;
          if (t !== type && !(grp && grp.includes(t)) && !(type === 55 && t === 230)) continue;
          total++;
          const d = Math.hypot(en.x - px, en.y - py);
          if (d < 200) near++;
          if (d < 600) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      itemSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // Item.MechSpawn（Item.cs:48982-49013）：同 item 三阈值拒绝
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.drops) {
          const d = o as ItemDrop;
          if (dropVanillaId(d) !== type) continue;
          total++;
          const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - px, d.y - py);
          if (dist < 300) near++;
          if (dist < 800) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      shootProjectile: (px: number, py: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: TrapShotKind, form = 0) => {
        this.entities.add(new TrapShot(px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind, form), 'projectiles');
        // 发射音：陷阱族=敲石近似；炮弹族由 TrapShot 首帧按原版播 Item14（Projectile.cs:21542/26830）；
        // 传送门弹 601 首帧无发射音（原版 AI 只有 alpha 段的门色拖尾尘）
        if (kind !== 'cannonball' && kind !== 'bunny' && kind !== 'snowball' && kind !== 'portalbolt') this.sfxAt('tink', px, py);
      },
      countProjectiles: (kind: 'spiky' | 'bunny', px: number, py: number, radius: number) => {
        let n = 0;
        for (const e of this.entities.projectiles) {
          if (e instanceof TrapShot && e.kind === kind && Math.hypot(e.x - px, e.y - py) < radius) n++;
        }
        return n;
      },
      doorOpen: (x: number, y: number) => {
        // 原版 HitWireSingle 10/11:随机方向 + 反向重试(WorldGen.OpenDoor)
        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
        if (!openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, dir)) return openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, -dir) ;
        return true;
      },
      doorClose: (x: number, y: number) => closeDoor(this.world.store, x, y),
      teleportPair: (a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => this.teleportPairImpl(a, b),
      relocateTownNpc: (group: 0 | 1, px: number, py: number) => {
        // 雕像 40/41 城镇 NPC 白名单(原版两组;此处按 NPC 图鉴顺序取前几个存在的)
        const groups: Array<Array<string>> = [
          ['guide', 'merchant', 'nurse', 'demolitionist', 'dyer', 'arms_dealer'],
          ['dryad', 'painter', 'golfer', 'zoologist', 'pirate', 'stylist'],
        ];
        for (const key of groups[group]) {
          const npc = this.entities.npcs.find((n) => (n as TownNPC).npcKey === key);
          if (npc) {
            npc.x = px - npc.w / 2;
            npc.y = py - npc.h - 1;
            return true;
          }
        }
        return false;
      },
      explode: (tx: number, ty: number, damage: number, radius?: number, destroyTiles?: boolean) =>
        this.explodeAt(tx, ty, damage, radius, destroyTiles),
      extractinatorPull: (ax: number, ay: number, blockType: number) =>
        this.extractinatorPullImpl(ax, ay, blockType),
      dropItemStack: (vid: number, stack: number, px: number, py: number) =>
        this.dropItemStackImpl(vid, stack, px, py),
      toast: (m: string) => this.cb.onToast(m),
      sfx: (n: string, x?: number, y?: number) => {
        if (x !== undefined && y !== undefined) this.sfxAt(n as SfxName, x, y);
        else this.sfx.play(n as SfxName);
      },
      // 广播盒（Wiring.cs:1177-1230）：锚点 2×2 内的告示牌文本 → 聊天广播。
      // 原版 NewTextMultiline 粉色多行——toast 单行近似（聊天多行渲染未接入）
      announce: (x: number, y: number) => {
        const sign = this.world.signs.find((s) => x >= s.x && x <= s.x + 1 && y >= s.y && y <= s.y + 1);
        if (sign && sign.text.trim()) this.cb.onToast(sign.text);
      },
      // 派对纪念碑（Wiring.cs:2037-2039 → BirthdayParty.ToggleManualParty）
      toggleParty: () => {
        toggleManualParty(this.partyState);
      },
    };
  }

  /** 传送器对(Wiring.cs:2314-2365):板上 48×48 判定框内实体双向搬迁 */
  private teleportPairImpl(a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) {
    // 太近的竖叠板拒绝(:2316)
    if (Math.abs(a[0] - b[0]) < 3 && a[1] > b[1] - 3 && a[1] < b[1]) return;
    const rect = (pad: [number, number]) => ({
      x: pad[0] * TILE, y: pad[1] * TILE - 48, w: 48, h: 48,
    });
    const hit = (r: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
      e.x < r.x + r.w && e.x + e.w > r.x && e.y < r.y + r.h && e.y + e.h > r.y;
    const ra = rect(a), rb = rect(b);
    const dx = rb.x - ra.x, dy = rb.y - ra.y;
    if (!this.wiring?.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration) {
      const p = this.player;
      if (!p.dead && hit(ra, p)) { p.x += dx; p.y += dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
      else if (!p.dead && hit(rb, p)) { p.x -= dx; p.y -= dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
    }
    for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
      if (hit(ra, e)) { e.x += dx; e.y += dy; }
      else if (hit(rb, e)) { e.x -= dx; e.y -= dy; }
    }
  }

  /** 爆炸不可炸 tile 表（Projectile.CanExploteTile cs:75764 1:1）：
   *  地牢砖族 tileDungeon{41,43,44,677-679}(Main.cs:7941) + 基础宝箱 BasicChest{21,467}
   *  (TileID.cs:315) + switch 黑名单{26 祭坛,88 柜子,121/122 史莱姆块?,150 蛛网? ,
   *  211 血月砖?,226 蜥蜴砖,237 蜥蜴祭坛,248-250 尖刺?,346/470/475/504 陷阱族,685/686} */
  private static readonly EXPLODE_NEVER = new Set([41, 43, 44, 677, 678, 679, 21, 467,
    26, 88, 121, 122, 150, 211, 226, 237, 248, 249, 250, 346, 470, 475, 504, 685, 686]);

```

// 器件态机族回归：八音盒 / 喷泉 / 天塔柱（SwitchMB/SwitchFountain/SwitchMonolith，
// WorldGen.cs:50977-51209）+ SceneMetrics 器件态扫描（:471-585）+ BGM 覆盖
// （Main.cs:11002-11405）+ 传送门落点（PortalHelper.cs:218-248）。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Wiring } from '../src/world/Wiring';
import {
  deviceTable, switchMB, switchFountain, switchMonolith,
  musicBoxStyleOf, fountainColorOf, monolithTypeOf, bloodMoonMonolithOf,
  MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC, type DeviceCtx,
} from '../src/world/wiring/devices';
import { scanScene } from '../src/world/SceneMetrics';
import { pickMusic, newMusicState, MUSIC } from '../src/data/Music';
import { placePortal, portalRegistry, clearPortals, portalOutingPoint } from '../src/entities/Portal';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

function makeCtx(): DeviceCtx {
  return {
    dropItem: () => {}, spawnNpc: () => true, mechSpawnOk: () => true, itemSpawnOk: () => true,
    shootProjectile: () => {}, countProjectiles: () => 0,
    doorOpen: () => true, doorClose: () => true, teleportPair: () => {},
    relocateTownNpc: () => true, explode: () => {}, toast: () => {}, sfx: () => {},
    extractinatorPull: () => null, dropItemStack: () => {},
    worldSurface: 100,
  };
}

function freshWiring() {
  const world = new World(80, 80, 7, 'open');
  return { world, w: new Wiring(world.store) };
}

/** 放 2×2 八音盒（tile 139）：style s → frameY = s*36 + 行*18，frameX = 列*18（off 带 <36） */
function placeMusicBox(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, style: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_139_musicboxes']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 2; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 2; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, cx * 18, style * 36 + cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

/** 放 2×4 喷泉（tile 207）：style s → frameX = s*36 + 列*18 */
function placeFountain(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, style: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_207_water_fountains']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 2; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 4; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, style * 36 + cx * 18, cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

/** 放 2×3 天塔柱（tile 410 style s / 480 / 509）：off 帧 frameY = 行*18 */
function placeMonolith(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, key: string, style = 0) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY[key]!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 2; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, style * 36 + cx * 18, cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

describe('八音盒 SwitchMB（WorldGen.cs:50977-51021）', () => {
  it('2×2 frameX ±36 翻转，任意格命中整块翻转', () => {
    const { w } = freshWiring();
    placeMusicBox(w, 10, 20, 3);
    expect(deviceTable(w, makeCtx(), 139, 11, 21)).toBe(true); // 右下格触发
    for (let cx = 0; cx < 2; cx++) {
      for (let cy = 0; cy < 2; cy++) {
        const i = w.st.idx(10 + cx, 20 + cy);
        expect(w.st.frameX[i]).toBe(36 + cx * 18); // +36 = on 带
      }
    }
    // 再触发一次回到 off
    deviceTable(w, makeCtx(), 139, 10, 20);
    expect(w.st.frameX[w.st.idx(10, 20)]).toBe(0);
  });

  it('开关态读取（SceneMetrics.cs:471-486）：off=-1 / on=style / 100=静音', () => {
    expect(musicBoxStyleOf(0, 0)).toBe(-1);
    expect(musicBoxStyleOf(36, 3 * 36)).toBe(3);
    expect(musicBoxStyleOf(36, 3 * 36 + 18)).toBe(3); // 块内第二行整除不变
    expect(musicBoxStyleOf(36, 100 * 36)).toBe(100);
  });

  it('style→MusicID 全表锚点（Main.cs:11002-11403）', () => {
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC).toHaveLength(100);
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC[0]).toBe(1);    // OverworldDay
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC[3]).toBe(6);    // TitleClassic（乱序 if-chain）
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC[36]).toBe(34);  // LunarPillars
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC[94]).toBe(92);  // Destroyer
    expect(MB_STYLE_TO_MUSIC[99]).toBe(103); // Skeletron
  });
});

describe('BGM 覆盖（Main.cs:11002-11405：DecideOnNewMusic 之后，最高优先级）', () => {
  const base = {
    scene: { townNPCCount: 0 } as never, isDay: true, bossMusic: 5, invasionMusic: 0,
    worldSurface: 200, rockLevel: 400, lavaLine: 900, maxTilesX: 4200,
    centerX: 2100 * 16, centerY: 3000, state: newMusicState(),
    weather: { storm: false, rain: false, windy: false, timeOfDay01: 0.5 },
  };
  it('音乐盒覆盖 Boss 曲；静音 style 100 → 0', () => {
    expect(pickMusic({ ...base, musicBox: 0 })).toBe(1);
    expect(pickMusic({ ...base, musicBox: 100 })).toBe(MUSIC.None);
    expect(pickMusic(base)).toBe(5); // 无盒 → Boss 曲

// 原版素材接入：按白名单从 terraria-assets 拷贝 PNG + 提取 TEdit 元数据 → public/sprites/vanilla.json
// 原版 Tiles 表无 .meta（不同于 Maples Unity 项目），按 textureGrid+frameGap 网格寻址：
//   帧 (col,row) 位于 (col*(gridX+gapX), row*(gridY+gapY))，帧尺寸 = textureGrid
// 用法：node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, copyFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = join(here, '..');
const ASSETS = join(ROOT, '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
const TEDIT_DATA = join(ROOT, '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');
const OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');
const OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla.json');
const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

/** 读 PNG IHDR（8 字节签名 + 4 长度 + 4 类型后即宽高，各 4 字节大端） */
function pngSize(file) {
  const b = readFileSync(file);
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));
const items = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'items.json'), 'utf8'));
const walls = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'walls.json'), 'utf8'));
const tileById = new Map(tiles.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
const itemById = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const wallById = new Map(walls.map((w) => [w.id, w]));

mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });

// 杂项单图素材（呼吸气泡等，非表驱动）+ 树木专用贴图（TEdit WorldRenderXna 逻辑）
const MISC = [
  'Bubble.png',
  // 原版云朵（Cloud.cs 常用 0-3；9-13 雨云/18-21 风暴云随天气系统后补）
  'Cloud_0.png', 'Cloud_1.png', 'Cloud_2.png', 'Cloud_3.png',
  // 树冠/树枝（style 0 起步；TEdit GetTreeTops/GetTreeBranches）
  ...Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => `Tree_Tops_${i}.png`),
  ...Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => `Tree_Branches_${i}.png`),
  // 各生物群系树干（TEdit GetTree → Images/Tiles_5_N）
...
    if (!existsSync(src)) continue;
    copyFileSync(src, join(UI_OUT_DIR, name));
    uiOut.files[name] = `vanilla-ui/${name}`;
    uiCopied++;
  }
}
// 盔甲贴图（纸娃娃装备渲染）：Armor_Head_{itemId}（20帧竖条）/ Armor_Legs_{itemId}（竖条）/
// Armor/Armor_{itemId}（9×4 复合网格，与玩家复合通道同构）
const armorFiles = readdirSync(ASSETS).filter((f) => /^Armor_(Head|Legs)_\d+\.png$/.test(f));
for (const f of armorFiles) {
  copyFileSync(join(ASSETS, f), join(UI_OUT_DIR, f));
  uiOut.files[f] = `vanilla-ui/${f}`;
  uiCopied++;
}
const armorDir = join(ASSETS, 'Armor');
if (existsSync(armorDir)) {
  for (const f of readdirSync(armorDir).filter((f) => /^Armor_\d+\.png$/.test(f))) {
    const key = `Armor_${f}`; // Armor/Armor_81.png → Armor_Armor_81.png（展平加前缀防撞名）
    copyFileSync(join(armorDir, f), join(UI_OUT_DIR, key));
    uiOut.files[key] = `vanilla-ui/${key}`;
    uiCopied++;
  }
}
writeFileSync(UI_OUT_JSON, JSON.stringify(uiOut));
console.log(`vanilla-ui: copied=${uiCopied}`);
if (uiMissing.length) console.warn('UI 缺失:', uiMissing.join(', '));

writeFileSync(OUT_JSON, JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`vanilla-atlas: tiles=${Object.keys(out.tiles).length} items=${Object.keys(out.items).length} walls=${Object.keys(out.walls).length} copied=${copied}`);
if (missing.length) console.warn('缺失（白名单有但文件/数据不存在）:', missing.join(', '));

}

let atlasPage = null, atlasX = 0, atlasY = 0, shelfH = 0, atlasIdx = -1;
const flushAtlas = () => {

  let png = null;

    continue;
  }
  if (!atlasPage) newAtlasPage();
  const iw = png.width, ih = png.height;
  if (atlasX + iw + GUTTER > ATLAS_W) { atlasX = GUTTER; atlasY += shelfH + GUTTER; shelfH = 0; }
  if (atlasY + ih + GUTTER > ATLAS_H) newAtlasPage();
  PNG.bitblt(png, atlasPage, 0, 0, iw, ih, atlasX, atlasY);

    ...base,
    icon: `vanilla/Item_Atlas_${atlasIdx}.png`,
    ix: atlasX, iy: atlasY, iw, ih,
  };
  atlasX += iw + GUTTER;
  if (ih > shelfH) shelfH = ih;
}
flushAtlas();
console.log(`item atlas pages: ${atlasIdx + 1}`);

    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };

  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
  private _worldPreloaded = false;
  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;

  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    const list = [...new Set(files)];
    let done = 0;
    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        this.vimages.set(f, img);
        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
      };
      img.onerror = () => resolve();
      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
    })));
  }

  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
    const files = new Set<string>();
    for (const id of tileSheets) {
      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
    }
    for (const id of wallIds) {
      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
    }
    return this.preloadFiles(files);
  }

  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */
  preloadMiscAndNpcs(): Promise<void> {
    return this.preloadFiles([
      ...VANILLA_MISC,
      ...Object.values(this.vanilla.npcs ?? {}).map((n) => n.sheet),
    ]);
  }

  /** 预载世界渲染所需原版表(全量,~750 张)。仅调试/兜底用;正常路径走
   *  preloadTileSheetsFor + preloadMiscAndNpcs 的出生点子集 */
  async preloadVanillaWorld(): Promise<void> {
    if (this._worldPreloaded) return;
    this._worldPreloaded = true;
    await Promise.all([
      this.preloadTileSheetsFor(
        Object.keys(this.vanilla.tiles).map(Number),
        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),
      ),
      this.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
    ]);
  }
  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      this.vimages.set(file, im);
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
    };
    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;

// 实体与 Game 交互的接口（避免循环依赖，只放类型）
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface GameHooks {
  world: World;
  /** 主角(投射物命中判定用) */
  player: import('./Player').Player;
  /** 敌人列表(投射物命中判定用) */
  enemies(): unknown[];
  /** 小动物列表(弹幕命中判定用;近战挥砍在 Game 内直接遍历桶) */
  critters(): unknown[];
  /** 在世界上生成掉落物（返回实体便于染色等后处理） */
  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown): void;
  /** 伤害数字飘字 */
  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color?: string): void;
  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义：箭扫过杂草/瓦罐 → Game 内部分流） */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 传送门弹 601 落门（PortalHelper.TryPlacingPortal + AddPortal；Game 实现入 props 桶） */
  placePortal?(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number): void;
  /** 敌人死亡 */
  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: import('../entities/Enemy').Enemy): void;
  /** 出生敌人 */
  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 粒子 */
  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed?: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 拾取物品后刷新 UI（掉落物实体调用） */
  notifyInventoryChanged(): void;
  /** 播放音效 */
  // 世界坐标声（原版 SoundEngine.PlaySound 带 x/y）：x/y 省略 = UI/玩家动作声不衰减
  playSfx(name: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void;
  playSfxFiles(files: string[], vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void; // 怪物专属声效（原版文件名列表）
  /** 拾取物品时在主角旁飘出物品名 */
  showPickupLabel(itemKey: string): void;
  /** 掉落系统补充上下文（灵魂/钥匙/风筝等条件）：Game 实现，缺省则这些条件按 false */
  dropContext?(): import('../drops/NpcDrops').DropExtras;
  /** 月事件上下文（南瓜月/霜月 wave-gate 掉落条件）：Game 实现，缺省按无事件（恒 false） */
  moonEventCtx?(): { kind: 1 | 2; wave: number } | null;
  /** 环境判定（Game.scene，SceneMetrics 每 15t 以玩家为中心刷新；Boss 暴怒度/石巨人
   *  脱庙加成读 zoneJungle/zoneTemple——原版 Main.player[target].ZoneJungle/ZoneLihzhardTemple） */
  scene?: import('../world/SceneMetrics').SceneFlags;
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Wiring } from '../src/world/Wiring';
import { deviceTable, type DeviceCtx } from '../src/world/wiring/devices';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { TrapShot } from '../src/entities/Dart';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

// 炮弹投射物 1:1 回归（Wiring.cs case 209/1301-1341 + case 212/1345-1418 →
// WorldGen.ShootFromCannon WorldGen.cs:50606-50718；Projectile 162/281/166 AI 与 Kill）
type Shot = { px: number; py: number; vx: number; vy: number; damage: number; kind: string; form: number };

function makeCtx() {
  const shots: Shot[] = [];
  let bunnyCount = 0;
  const ctx: DeviceCtx = {
    dropItem: () => {}, spawnNpc: () => true, mechSpawnOk: () => true, itemSpawnOk: () => true,
    shootProjectile: (px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind, form = 0) => shots.push({ px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind, form }),
    countProjectiles: (kind) => (kind === 'bunny' ? bunnyCount : 0),
    doorOpen: () => true, doorClose: () => true, teleportPair: () => {},
    relocateTownNpc: () => true, explode: () => {}, toast: () => {}, sfx: () => {},
    extractinatorPull: () => null, dropItemStack: () => {},
    worldSurface: 100,
  };
  return { ctx, shots, setBunnyCount: (n: number) => { bunnyCount = n; } };
}

/** 放置一座 4×3 大炮（tile 209）：frameX = style*72 + col*18，frameY = angle*54 + row*18 */
function placeCannon(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, style: number, angle: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_209_cannon']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 4; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, style * 72 + cx * 18, angle * 54 + cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

/** 放置一座 3×3 雪球发射器（tile 212）：frameX = facing*54 + col*18，frameY = row*18 */
function placeSnowLauncher(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, facing: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_212_snowballlauncher']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 3; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, facing * 54 + cx * 18, cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

function freshWiring() {
  const world = new World(60, 60, 7, 'open');
  return new Wiring(world.store);
}

    expect(s.vy).toBeCloseTo(0, 6);

  });

    expect(shots[0]!.vy).toBeCloseTo(-14, 6);

// ---- TrapShot 炮弹族弹体（Projectile 162/281/166 SetDefaults + AI + Kill）----

function makeGame() {
  const world = new World(40, 40, 7, 'open');
  const hits: Array<{ dmg: number; x: number; y: number }> = [];
  const playerHits: number[] = [];
  const enemy = {
    x: 40, y: 60, w: 20, h: 20, dead: false,
    hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number) => { hits.push({ dmg: d, x: kx, y: ky }); return true; },
  };
  const game = {
    world,
    player: { x: -500, y: -500, w: 20, h: 20, dead: false, cx: -490, damage: (d: number) => playerHits.push(d) },
    enemies: () => [enemy],
    critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {}, spawnParticles: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
  } as unknown as GameHooks;
  return { game, hits, playerHits, enemy };
}

private fireworkUpdate(game: GameHooks): void {

}

   *  每 3 tick 扫视口，按 sheet 查规则表发射。与 emitTorchSparks 共用节流（火把独立保留 1:1 实现）。 */
  private emitTileParticles() {
    if (this.tickCount % 3 !== 0) return;
    const st = this.world.store;
        if (!rules) continue;
        const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
        for (const rule of rules) {
          if (Math.random() >= rule.prob) continue;
          if (rule.fx0 !== undefined && fx !== rule.fx0) continue;
          if (rule.fy0 !== undefined && fy !== rule.fy0) continue;
          if (rule.pred && !rule.pred(fx, fy)) continue;
          if (rule.lightGate !== undefined && this.lighting.lightAt(x, y)[0] <= rule.lightGate) continue;
          const [smin, smax] = rule.size ?? [1.5, 3];
          const life = Math.round((rule.life ?? 40) * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6));
          const rise = rule.rise ?? 0.4;
          this.particles.push({
            x: x * TILE + (rule.dx ?? 8) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4,
            y: y * TILE + (rule.dy ?? 0) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4,
            vx: (rule.sideways ?? 0) * (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.2,
            vy: -rise * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6),
            life, maxLife: life,
            color: rule.color,
            size: smin + Math.random() * (smax - smin),
            grav: rule.grav ?? (rise < 0 ? 0.1 : -0.005),
            damp: rule.damp ?? 0.985,

```

        if (this.tp[0][0] === tx && this.tp[0][1] === y + halfOff) return;
        this.tp[1] = [tx, y + halfOff];

        let px = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
        if (px > 1) px -= 2;
        const ox = x - px;
        this.skipWire(ox, py); this.skipWire(ox, py + 1);
        this.skipWire(ox + 1, py); this.skipWire(ox + 1, py + 1);
        const cells: Array<[number, number]> = [[ox, py + 1], [ox + 1, py + 1], [ox, py], [ox + 1, py]];
        if (sheet === 142) {
          for (let k = 0; k < 4 && this.numInPump < 19; k++) {
            this.inPumpX[this.numInPump] = cells[k][0];
            this.inPumpY[this.numInPump] = cells[k][1];
            this.numInPump++;
          }
        } else {
          for (let k = 0; k < 4 && this.numOutPump < 19; k++) {
            this.outPumpX[this.numOutPump] = cells[k][0];
            this.outPumpY[this.numOutPump] = cells[k][1];
            this.numOutPump++;
          }
        }
        return;
      }
      case 429: { // 彩线灯泡:按当前色翻对应位(:1325-1357)
        const q = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
        let num53 = 0, on = false;
        switch (this.currentWireColor) {
          case 1: num53 = 18; on = !(q % 2 >= 1); break;
          case 2: num53 = 72; on = !(q % 8 >= 4); break;
          case 3: num53 = 36; on = !(q % 4 >= 2); break;
          case 4: num53 = 144; on = !(q % 16 >= 8); break;
        }
        st.setTile(x, y, type, st.frameX[i] + (on ? num53 : -num53), st.frameY[i]);
        return;
      }
    }

137	  dead = false;
138	
139	  /** 全透明贴图缓存(xnb 解包异常的 187/654 等):检出一次后走色块回退,避免隐形弹幕 */
140	  private static blankTex = new Set<string>();
141	  private static okTex = new Set<string>();
142	  private static isBlank(r: Renderer, path: string): boolean {
143	    if (TrapShot.blankTex.has(path)) return true;
144	    if (TrapShot.okTex.has(path)) return false;
145	    const img = r.atlas?.vimages.get(path);
146	    let blank = true;
147	    if (img) {
148	      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
149	      c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
150	      const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
151	      cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
152	      const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
153	      for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) { if (d[i] > 30) { blank = false; break; } }
154	    }
155	    (blank ? TrapShot.blankTex : TrapShot.okTex).add(path);
156	    return blank;
157	  }

170	  }

191	  }

 "new_string": "    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);\n  }\n\n  /** 定向粒子爆发（GameHooks.spawnBurst）：烟花爆花/喷泉/马桶水花的原版 dust\n   *  带初速方向与轴向压缩（Projectile.cs Kill/AI 各循环的速度档与 x/y 乘数）。\n   *  角度 = dir ± spread；速度系数 0.85+rand*0.2 对齐原版喷泉的\n   *  `spinningpoint *= 0.85 + NextDouble()*0.2`（Projectile.cs:29388-29390 等） */\n  spawnBurst(\n    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed: number,\n    dir: number, spread: number,\n    opts: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; xMul?: number; yMul?: number } = {},\n  ) {\n    const base = opts.life ?? 40;\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n      const a = dir + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread;\n      const sp = speed * (0.85 + Math.random() * 0.2);\n      const life = Math.round(base * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.4));\n      this.particles.push({\n        x, y,\n        vx: Math.cos(a) * sp * (opts.xMul ?? 1),\n        vy: Math.sin(a) * sp * (opts.yMul ?? 1),\n        life, maxLife: life, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 2,\n        damp: opts.damp, grav: opts.grav,\n      });\n    }\n    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);\n  }"
}

 "new_string": "/** 马桶烟花（Wiring.cs:1498-1509）：tile 497 与 tile 15 马桶样式（frameY/40==1 或 20）共用。\n *  num68 = j - frameY%40/18（锚点上格）；SkipWire 两格；CheckMech(锚点, 60) → 弹 733\n *  ToiletEffect：静态水花发射器（速度 0 / damage 0），位于 (i*16+8, num68*16+12) */\nfunction fireworkToilet(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {\n  const st = w.st;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  const num68 = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 40 / 18);\n  w.skipCell(x, num68); w.skipCell(x, num68 + 1); // :1501-1502\n  if (!w.checkMech(x, num68, 60)) return;          // :1503（60t 冷却）\n  ctx.shootProjectile(x * 16 + 8, num68 * 16 + 12, 0, 0, 0, 'toiletPop'); // :1505\n}\n\n/** 巨石雕像 531(Wiring.cs:1747-1762) */"

/** 马桶烟花（Wiring.cs:1498-1509）：tile 497 与 tile 15 马桶样式（frameY/40==1 或 20）共用。
 *  num68 = j - frameY%40/18（锚点上格）；SkipWire 两格；CheckMech(锚点, 60) → 弹 733
 *  ToiletEffect：静态水花发射器（速度 0 / damage 0），位于 (i*16+8, num68*16+12) */
function fireworkToilet(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);

    case 386: case 387: { // 活板门开/关互换(ShiftTrapdoor;帧近似)

      const toOpen = sheet === 387;
      const target = TILE_BY_KEY[toOpen ? openK : closedK] ?? 0;
      if (!target) return true;
      const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
      for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + 2; ix++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(ix, y)) continue;
        const ii = st.idx(ix, y);
        w.skipCell(ix, y);
        st.setTile(ix, y, target, (ix - ox) * 18, st.frameY[ii]);
      }
      return true;
